results[best_lambda]['mean_brier']\n\nprint(f\"\\nBest lambda: {best_lambda}\")\nprint(f\"Baseline (lambda=0) Brier: {results[0.0]['mean_brier']:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Best lambda Brier: {results[best_lambda]['mean_brier']:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Improvement: {improvement_over_baseline:.6f}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"\n"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

With interactions\n    model_int = LogisticRegression(C=best_C, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000, random_state=42)\n    model_int.fit(X_train_int, y_train)\n    y_pred_int = model_int.predict_proba(X_val_int)[:, 1]\n    brier_int = brier_score_loss(y_val, y_pred_int)\n    interaction_fold_scores.append(brier_int)\n\nraw_fold_scores = np.array(raw_fold_scores)\ninteraction_fold_scores = np.array(interaction_fold_scores)\n\nprint(f\"\\nRaw model Brier scores per fold: {raw_fold_scores}\")\nprint(f\"With interactions Brier scores per fold: {interaction_fold_scores}\")\nprint(f\"Raw mean: {raw_fold_scores.mean():.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Interactions mean: {interaction_fold_scores.mean():.6f}\")\n\n# Paired t-test: compare fold scores\n# We want to test if interactions help (lower is better Brier score)\n# Differences: raw - interactions (positive means interactions are better)\ndifferences = raw_fold_scores - interaction_fold_scores\nprint(f\"Differences (raw - interactions): {differences}\")\nprint(f\"Mean difference: {differences.mean():.6f}\")\n\n# Paired t-test\nt_stat, p_value = stats.ttest_rel(raw_fold_scores, interaction_fold_scores)\nprint(f\"Paired t-test: t={t_stat:.6f}, p-value={p_value:.6f}\")\n\n# interaction_helps is true only if t > 2.0\n# Since lower Brier is better, we want interactions to have lower scores\n# So we test if raw_fold_scores > interaction_fold_scores (i.e., t > 2.0)\ninteraction_helps = t_stat > 2.0\nprint(f\"Does interaction help (t > 2.0)? {interaction_helps}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"\n"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

With interactions\n    model_int = LogisticRegression(C=best_C, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000, random_state=42)\n    model_int.fit(X_train_int, y_train)\n    y_pred_int = model_int.predict_proba(X_val_int)[:, 1]\n    brier_int = brier_score_loss(y_val, y_pred_int)\n    interaction_fold_scores.append(brier_int)\n\nraw_fold_scores = np.array(raw_fold_scores)\ninteraction_fold_scores = np.array(interaction_fold_scores)\n\nprint(f\"\\nRaw model Brier scores per fold: {raw_fold_scores}\")\nprint(f\"With interactions Brier scores per fold: {interaction_fold_scores}\")\nprint(f\"Raw mean: {raw_fold_scores.mean():.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Interactions mean: {interaction_fold_scores.mean():.6f}\")\n\n# Paired t-test: compare fold scores\n# We want to test if interactions help (lower is better Brier score)\n# Differences: raw - interactions (positive means interactions are better)\ndifferences = raw_fold_scores - interaction_fold_scores\nprint(f\"Differences (raw - interactions): {differences}\")\nprint(f\"Mean difference: {differences.mean():.6f}\")\n\n# Paired t-test\nt_stat, p_value = stats.ttest_rel(raw_fold_scores, interaction_fold_scores)\nprint(f\"Paired t-test: t={t_stat:.6f}, p-value={p_value:.6f}\")\n\n# interaction_helps is true only if t > 2.0\n# Since lower Brier is better, we want interactions to have lower scores\n# So we test if raw_fold_scores > interaction_fold_scores (i.e., t > 2.0)\ninteraction_helps = t_stat > 2.0\nprint(f\"Does interaction help (t > 2.0)? {interaction_helps}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Paired t-test: t=5.802122, p-value=0.004389"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

X_min) / (X_max - X_min) - 1\n    \n    # Quantize\n    levels = 2 ** n_bits\n    X_quant = np.round(X_norm * (levels / 2 - 1)) / (levels / 2 - 1)\n    \n    return X_quant\n\n# Test all configurations\nsparsities = [20, 40, 60]\nquantizations = [8, 4]\n\nconfigs = []\nfor sparsity in sparsities:\n    for quant in quantizations:\n        configs.append({\n            'sparsity': sparsity,\n            'quantization': quant,\n            'X_processed': None,\n            'mean_brier': None,\n        })\n\n# Dense model baseline\ndense_model_brier = results[10.0]['mean_brier']  # Using best lambda=10.0\nprint(f\"Dense model (lambda=10.0) Brier: {dense_model_brier:.6f}\")\n\n# Process and evaluate each configuration\nfor config in configs:\n    X_sparse = apply_sparsity(X, config['sparsity'])\n    X_processed = apply_quantization(X_sparse, config['quantization'])\n    \n    fold_scores = []\n    for train_idx, val_idx in kf.split(X_processed):\n        X_train, X_val = X_processed[train_idx], X_processed[val_idx]\n        y_train, y_val = y[train_idx], y[val_idx]\n        \n        model = LogisticRegression(C=best_C, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000, random_state=42)\n        model.fit(X_train, y_train)\n        \n        y_pred = model.predict_proba(X_val)[:, 1]\n        brier = brier_score_loss(y_val, y_pred)\n        fold_scores.append(brier)\n    \n    config['mean_brier'] = np.mean(fold_scores)\n    config['X_processed'] = X_processed\n    \n    performance_ratio = config['mean_brier'] / dense_model_brier\n    print(f\"Sparsity={config['sparsity']}%, Quantization={config['quantization']}bit: \"\n          f\"Brier={config['mean_brier']:.6f}, \"\n          f\"Ratio to dense={performance_ratio:.4f}\")\n\n# Find best config - (best_config['mean_brier'] - dense_model_brier) / dense_model_brier) * 100:.2f}%\")\n\nbest_config_str = f\"{best_config['sparsity']}-sparsity-{best_config['quantization']}-bit\"\nprint(f\"\\nBest config string: {best_config_str}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"\n"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

X_min) / (X_max - X_min) - 1\n    \n    # Quantize\n    levels = 2 ** n_bits\n    X_quant = np.round(X_norm * (levels / 2 - 1)) / (levels / 2 - 1)\n    \n    return X_quant\n\n# Test all configurations\nsparsities = [20, 40, 60]\nquantizations = [8, 4]\n\nconfigs = []\nfor sparsity in sparsities:\n    for quant in quantizations:\n        configs.append({\n            'sparsity': sparsity,\n            'quantization': quant,\n            'X_processed': None,\n            'mean_brier': None,\n        })\n\n# Dense model baseline\ndense_model_brier = results[10.0]['mean_brier']  # Using best lambda=10.0\nprint(f\"Dense model (lambda=10.0) Brier: {dense_model_brier:.6f}\")\n\n# Process and evaluate each configuration\nfor config in configs:\n    X_sparse = apply_sparsity(X, config['sparsity'])\n    X_processed = apply_quantization(X_sparse, config['quantization'])\n    \n    fold_scores = []\n    for train_idx, val_idx in kf.split(X_processed):\n        X_train, X_val = X_processed[train_idx], X_processed[val_idx]\n        y_train, y_val = y[train_idx], y[val_idx]\n        \n        model = LogisticRegression(C=best_C, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000, random_state=42)\n        model.fit(X_train, y_train)\n        \n        y_pred = model.predict_proba(X_val)[:, 1]\n        brier = brier_score_loss(y_val, y_pred)\n        fold_scores.append(brier)\n    \n    config['mean_brier'] = np.mean(fold_scores)\n    config['X_processed'] = X_processed\n    \n    performance_ratio = config['mean_brier'] / dense_model_brier\n    print(f\"Sparsity={config['sparsity']}%, Quantization={config['quantization']}bit: \"\n          f\"Brier={config['mean_brier']:.6f}, \"\n          f\"Ratio to dense={performance_ratio:.4f}\")\n\n# Find best config - smallest weights, keeping (100-sparsity_percent)% of features\"\"\"\n    X_sparse = X_data.copy()\n    n_features = X_sparse.shape[1]\n    n_to_zero = int(n_features * sparsity_percent / 100.0)\n    \n    # For each sample, zero out the smallest n_to_zero features (by absolute value)\n    for i in range(X_sparse.shape[0]):\n        # Get indices of smallest absolute values\n        abs_vals = np.abs(X_sparse[i, :])\n        indices_to_zero = np.argsort(abs_vals)[:n_to_zero]\n        X_sparse[i, indices_to_zero] = 0\n    \n    return X_sparse\n\ndef apply_quantization(X_data, n_bits):\n    \"\"\"Quantize features to n_bits precision\"\"\"\n    X_quant = X_data.copy()\n    # Simple uniform quantization: map to [-2^(n_bits-1), 2^(n_bits-1)-1] range\n    # First normalize to [-1, 1]\n    X_min = np.min(X_quant)\n    X_max = np.max(X_quant)\n    X_norm = 2 * (X_quant - X_min) / (X_max - X_min) - 1\n    \n    # Quantize\n    levels = 2 ** n_bits\n    X_quant = np.round(X_norm * (levels / 2 - 1)) / (levels / 2 - 1)\n    \n    return X_quant\n\n# Test all configurations\nsparsities = [20, 40, 60]\nquantizations = [8, 4]\n\nconfigs = []\nfor sparsity in sparsities:\n    for quant in
